**CONFIDENTIAL**

A3-2/2 (Unidentified Flying Object) observation reports have been increasing globally in recent years, with various governments and organizations taking notice. These sightings range from bright, disc-shaped objects moving erratically to more subtle, long-duration tracks on radar. While some cases remain unexplained, many can be attributed to natural phenomena, aircraft, or weather effects. It is crucial to maintain a balanced approach, avoiding both over-excitement and skepticism, as the truth may lie in a combination of factors. Further investigation and transparency are essential to understanding these mysterious events.

--- seg-aaf70f909b86dcd20cd4c523 [needs-review] ---
Residents claim
to have seen UFO in MS
RUBENS VALENTE
from Folha Agency, "in Campo Grande"
--- seg-ec56381c4cc863b45f91f011 [needs-review] ---
Residents of the municipality of
Chapadão do Sul (280 km from Campo Grande) say they saw an unidentified flying object last afternoon.

Radio Phoenix, in Chapadão do Sul, received 20 calls yesterday morning from people claiming to have seen the supposed flying saucer, according to the station's manager, Acari Dias Coelho.

The most detailed testimony is that of state tax agent Francisco Fetter. He said he reported the object for three minutes, around 5:50 p.m. (Brasília time), still under the sun's light, over the tax post where he works, on the highway between the cities of Chapadão do Sul (MS) and Chapadão do Céu (GO).

According to Fetter, the object had the shape of a pot lid in the vertical position and emitted a very strong light, similar to that produced by welding machines. He estimated the object's size to be that of a soccer field.

The UFO made no noise and had a reddish color. Then, it headed north in the state.

About two minutes after its departure, Fetter made radio contact with the nearest tax post in the north, about 80 km away.

When he answered the radio, tax agent Mozar Menezes declared he was observing the object over the post.

Four tourists who visited the Emas National Park in Goiás also told Fetter they had seen an object with the same characteristics.

They were carrying cameras and video recorders, but had been "unresponsive" and did not make any images.

"It was a spectacular light, marvelous," Fetter said. The agent claimed to be "excited and not able to speak."

Argentina
The presence of supposed flying saucers was also observed in Argentina by ufology experts at the end of last week. Three suspicious objects were detected in the sky of Santa Rosa, in the province of La Pampa. The information was disclosed online by scholar Oscar Alfredo "Mario". According to him, photographs made during the observation of the phenomenon may confirm if they were, indeed, UFOs. Mario stated that the photographs will be revealed in the coming days. The appearance of the flying objects was observed on Saturday night and early Sunday morning.
